## 14 November 2026 | N12 hosted compression diagnosis

Two exact-source Windows fixture runs reconciled all 859 observed `FSCTL_SET_COMPRESSION` requests. Every issuing thread resolved to Windows Performance Recorder (`wpr.exe`), and every request completed successfully outside protected roots. The protected and outside controls passed in both runs. Strict coverage remains **BLOCKED** by other mutations, including 48 and 41 write path gaps respectively. N12 remains **PARTIAL** pending full native capture and attribution. ## 14 November 2026 | P4-83 read-only USN journal correction

[Source commit `dc3b742`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/commit/dc3b74202a10320da580a91a5f8ad8851936cc65) correctly classifies Event 23 `FSCTL_READ_USN_JOURNAL (0x000900BB)` as a read-only query. The exact-source [N12 Windows run #43563056277](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/43563056277) passed 50 focused tests and decoded 376/376 rundown records. The [broader P4 run #43563056278](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/43563056278) passed all three jobs: 235 Python tests, 78 Node checks, TypeScript/Vite build, 13 Chromium scenarios, synthetic Windows preservation checks and a second 340/340 rundown integration. Both pre-opened controls passed through the strict reducer. The prior `0x000900BB` unresolved event family disappeared, while unknown FSCTLs remain fail-closed.

**N12 is still PARTIAL and global strict coverage is BLOCKED.** Unrelated protected-root writes/resizes and unresolved `other` mutation families remain, including missing path/process lifetime evidence and `FSCTL_SET_COMPRESSION`. No full PilotODIN startup-to-exit attribution or live combined Sysmon/ETW calibration was performed. P4 remains ACTIVE; R03 is NOT TESTED; R06 remains historical STRICT FAIL; P5 is NOT AUTHORISED; P8 is NOT CERTIFIED. See [P4-83](./P4_83_N12_READ_USN_JOURNAL_CLASSIFICATION_2026-11-14.md).

## 12 November 2026 | P4-54 independent real-data fixed-origin calendar baseline: NO-FIT audit

- **Meaningful negative historical result:** public user-uploaded daily UCI Bike Sharing data (731 rows) under the predeclared P4-52 first-584 vs last-147 chronological periods. A reference rule using development-only **month × workingday mean** has 147-day holdout **MAE 2,353.14**, **worse** than the development-only constant median **2,259.16** (by 93.98/day, ~4.16%). Month-only 2,387.14, month×weekday 2,375.19, weekday-only 2,287.17; these diagnostics do **not** justify holdout-based model selection. Computed independently in JS on the checked-in fixture and Python/pandas on the uploaded CSV, no ML fit/experiment API.
- **Critical comparability finding:** 24 development month×workingday categories, minimum group count 9, final holdout unknown groups **0**; but expanding CV folds had respectively **141/146, 61/146, 0/146** dates with unseen month×workingday groups, forcing fallback means. Mean daily rentals rose from **4,153.60** development to **5,897.80** holdout; Sep-Dec 2011 **3,609.99** vs 2012 **5,686.81**. These are period differences, not proven causes or model predictions.
- **Decision boundary:** current rolling previous-day/7-day/week-ago references are conditional on report latency, and **not equivalent** to a 147-day fixed-origin forecast. P4-54 documents frozen horizon, timestamp/as-of availability, cold-category/2013 unknown-year risk, holdout reuse concern and business-owner dependency before any separately approved research fit. See [P4-54 report](./P4_54_PUBLIC_BIKE_FIXED_ORIGIN_CALENDAR_BASELINE_2026-11-12.md). **No new source-code change, CI, native build, model fitting or owner original-data access.**

## 12 November 2026 | P4-43 read-only original preservation stability, exact-source Windows CI PASS

- **P4-R06 source-confirmed false-confidence condition:** `qa/p4_native/Compare_P4_Original_Storage.ps1` previously scanned the independently verified pre-QA backup and current original app-data tree only once, then calculated file and folder change counts and EXE integrity. A source mutation after the initial current/backup scan but before the output could leave stale counts and even a **FULL_FINGERPRINT_MATCH** verdict. This was a code-level comparison vulnerability, **not evidence of a new owner-data write**.
- **Bounded read-only fix:** [`0ff02440`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/commit/0ff0244093b5be05f7bcb0eb746d37811303c650) adds a second complete read-only scan of **both backup and original-data tree**, plus a second original EXE SHA256. The comparator rejects with an exception and **does not emit a falsely stable result** when file paths, content hashes, sizes, empty-directory membership or executable digest change between the two sequential observations. The new `sequential_rescan_consistent=true` field is emitted only for stable comparisons alongside `snapshot_is_atomic=false`. The historical P4-22 v1 file-only baseline remains untouched. Two sequential reads are **not** an atomic filesystem snapshot and cannot establish past writer identity or guarantee no future write.
- **Deterministic disposable Windows regression:** The test fixture deliberately mutates either a **current original-data synthetic file** or an **independent backup synthetic file** when the initial original EXE hash is collected after the first tree scan. Such post-scan changes previously could return a false full match; they now **fail closed**, and restoring clean disposable files yields a limited full match. Synthetic tests remain restricted to ephemeral Windows CI runner files. Three earlier [#43148792745](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/43148792745), [#43148981103](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/43148981103) and [#43149155782](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/43149155782) **FAILED** due to test-harness hash interception scope/PowerShell 5.1 compatibility. Corrections [`78bc25c7`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/commit/78bc25c7e73f37b39f9b7cf3fe043cf532f075fb), [`9304f217`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/commit/9304f2170f770247a3551485ee6fec63c59dd63c) and [`e6803505`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/commit/e680350500e1634b0599dc3926068439e37bb48b) isolated the interception to a disposable child scope. The failed runs are preserved as failures and **not** retroactively counted PASS.
- **Final exact-source verification:** [P4 GitHub Actions #43149480761](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/43149480761) **COMPLETED SUCCESS 3/3** at source `e680350500e1634b0599dc3926068439e37bb48b`. Logs: **150 Python tests PASS, 40 Node tests PASS/0 fail, TypeScript/Vite build PASS, 9 disposable real-HTTP Chromium tests PASS, original storage and WebView synthetic Windows safeguards PASS on PowerShell 7.6.9 and Windows PowerShell 5.1.20348.5773**. No app logic changes outside the two read-only QA PowerShell scripts; no installer, owner dataset, model fitting or owner machine action.
- **Binding gates still NO-GO:** Earlier owner-original EXE hash and independent backup matched, and original protected project `data/` showed zero detected differences. However, **strict entire original app-data tree preservation FAIL** remains (320 backup vs 346 observed files, 74 WebView/cache *candidates*, historical writer UNKNOWN). The revised comparator has **not run on owner data**; cannot assert recovery, causal attribution or historical folder provenance. Latest-source physical Windows WebView2 parity, 8 GB offline Qwen and independently supported real-business Gate C remain UNVERIFIED. P4-R01 through R06 OPEN, owner refused risk deferral. **P4 ACTIVE; P5 NOT AUTHORISED; P8 NOT CERTIFIED.** Owner prohibits P4–P7 intermediate installer builds/downloads/installations, P8 final packaging requires new owner approval.
- **Next priority:** Advance independent, real-owner P4 closure evidence when feasible rather than treating more source-level checks as a substitute. Source CI is now functional under isolated P4 concurrency group, despite older stuck Actions entries. Focused report: [P4-43](./P4_43_ORIGINAL_PRESERVATION_SCAN_STABILITY_2026-11-12.md).

## 11 November 2026 | P4-39 numeric categorical non-finite screening bypass fixed; exact-source CI PASS

- **Current-source/synthetic finding:** `backend/target_opportunities.py::_candidate` previously used `pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(series)` directly for target infinity/complex checks and predictor finite-value checks. This predicate is false for a pandas numeric **CategoricalDtype** Series, so `pd.Categorical([+inf,-inf]*40)` could bypass numeric-label validation, or numeric categorical predictors could falsely appear to vary through infinities. This was reproduced with pandas dtype probes and source inspection; no owner-data incident is claimed.

No file writes, input DataFrame/category mutation, data cleaning, automatic target selection, ML training or packaging. Tests cover three invalid categorical targets, three invalid categorical predictors, finite numeric category control, and complex-valued category rejection, with manual/queue review and original-data immutability assertions.

Earlier independent original production EXE+backup integrity matches and zero protected original `data/` changes remain historical evidence, but **strict full original app-data tree preservation FAIL** persists (320 baseline vs 346 current; 74 browser/cache *candidates*, historic writer UNKNOWN). Latest-source physical Windows UI, owner offline Qwen/8 GB and independent real-business Gate C remain NOT RETESTED / UNVERIFIED. **P4-R01–R06 OPEN; P4 ACTIVE; P5 NOT AUTHORISED; P8 NOT CERTIFIED.** [Focused P4-39 report](./P4_39_NUMERIC_CATEGORICAL_NONFINITE_SCREENING_2026-11-11.md).

## 11 November 2026 | P4-37 non-finite numeric predictor structural false-positive corrected; CI PASS

- **Source-confirmed P4 screening defect:** `backend/target_opportunities.py::_candidate` already rejected non-finite **numeric target labels**, but the predictor variation check counted infinities as distinct nonmissing feature values. ## 9 November 2026 | P4-20 labelled-row predictor screening defect source-fixed, exact CI 108/28/8 PASS

- **P4 risk-first instruction:** User said `next`; no approval to accept residual risk/close P4 or start P5. [P4-20 scope and evidence ledger](./P4_20_LABELLED_ROW_PREDICTOR_OVERLAP_SAFETY_AUDIT_2026-11-09.md).
- **Source-confirmed structural false readiness:** `_candidate` previously treated an input predictor as usable if it varied anywhere in the dataset, even when all varying observations occurred only in rows with **missing target labels**. On an 80-row synthetic `churn` frame with 40 observed labels and 40 missing, the sole `feature` could be missing or constant throughout *all labelled rows* and still count as a predictor. Four new synthetic cases cover both false-positive patterns, manual fail-closed path and a valid partially labelled control.

For datasets larger than 30k rows, a local ML row-cap **caution** is preserved without asserting a counted full labelled population. The separate manually controlled selected-target inspection remains available with P4-13 bounds, but also does not approve or start training.

## 5 November 2026 | P2 superseded Deliver export reliability fix

| --- | --- | --- |
| **P0** Core A2/A3 development | Substantial owner-observed workflows and focused Windows automated checks; **development journey accepted** for the observed checkpoint | Broader production/offline release certification not granted |
| **P1** Import, Quality, project/UI, resource policy | Owner-native Quality lifecycle/restart display, project manager, unsaved-change Stay/Switch, keyboard; owner-reported scaling/Snap/Narrator/reduced motion; native resource mode/save/RAM display. Hosted integration and contracts separately PASS. | **CLOSED development scope (5 Nov)** with tracked unverified native forced-failure/multi-window and independent measurement limitations; P8 release gates remain OPEN. |
| **P2** Deliver / PDF / reports | Scoped report, immutable revision, Save As, font resource, Unicode DevQA and standalone frozen font proof | **CURRENT ACTIVE: REVIEW REQUIRED**. Production Unicode PDF **VISUAL_FAIL** / not accepted; real sidecar resource readiness/inclusion, mixed-script renderer and independent release font SHA/licence owner decision remain OPEN. |
| **P3** Semantic understanding | First shared semantic profile and focused verification | Partial. Broader semantic accuracy and real native UX validation |
| **P4** Target intelligence | Focused ranking, business context, timing/provenance and two-axis evidence | Partial. Real-world decision value and integrated experience; **explicit user target choice required** |
| **P5** Durable ML/resource task controls | Focused low-memory slot, factual progress, soft cooperative cancellation and interruption safeguards | Partial. General queue/retry/crash resume, process-level hard-stop and combined AI/ML scheduling |
| **P6** Master Analyst / Master Scientist | Architecture and research; some reusable foundations | **Not product-accepted**. Build deterministic end-to-end Master workflows with approval and validation |
| **P7** Autonomous local ODIN / orchestration | Preliminary typed-tool/controller and resource foundations | **Not accepted as genuine autonomous offline capability**. Grounded multi-step inference, safety, repair, recoverable task control |
| **P8** Clean offline Windows Build F | Desktop/sidecar/installer source and selected older DEV proof exist | **NOT RELEASE CERTIFIED**: clean install, bundled licensed local AI/runtime, real 8 GB coexistence, security/privacy, accessibility, rollback and E-01–E-24 |

## Non-negotiable acceptance boundaries
- A green Node test is **not** a native Windows test. Simulated 150% DPR is **not** physical Windows scaling.
- A source/frozen resource proof is **not** production Unicode rendering or licence approval.
- A working desktop DEV window is **not** clean installed offline 1.0 acceptance.
- A read-only RAM inventory is **not** a successful offline Qwen + ML concurrent benchmark. A prior ~8 GB Windows snapshot showed serious memory pressure, but did not prove infeasibility.
- Historic P1 75–80% and overall ~40–45% figures were illustrative judgements, **not auditable earned-value KPIs**. This ledger therefore uses evidence statuses, not official percentage-complete claims.

## Latest P1 archived-project HTTP write guard (4 November 2026)
At tested [`1ac2587920cd`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/commit/1ac2587920cd0e482102ce96a24c0b739ad220fe), state-changing HTTP requests for archived projects are blocked by a fixed SQL ownership lookup, including indirect experiment, investigation and report endpoints. 409 asks the user to restore the project. Desktop secret authentication occurs before the ownership lookup. These changes are a **request preflight** only and do not supply commit-time atomicity or durable deletion exclusion.

Verification: [14/14 backend pytest](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/41840614063) on the unchanged backend source ancestor; [145/145 frontend Node + build + 5/5 Chromium](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/41840976435) and [4/4 real Uvicorn/Chromium scenarios](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/41840976488) at final source SHA, all using synthetic disposable projects. No permanent deletion, installed app change, native WebView2 acceptance, physical scaling or Narrator. P1 **OPEN**.

## Latest P1 Quality transaction-bound archive fence (4 November 2026)
Tested [`8a4c66a262fd`](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/commit/8a4c66a262fd7525c45180b007989473aecaa110): Quality proposal receipts, committed approvals, legacy decisions, undo and restore now re-check project archival status **inside their `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` write transaction**. A preloaded project object cannot bypass the check. SQLite serializes the covered Quality write against a concurrent lifecycle archive.

[Backend #41852781940](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/41852781940) **16/16 synthetic pytest PASS** with explicit archived-first and synchronized write-first race checks on unchanged backend ancestor. [Real HTTP #41853026518](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/41853026518) **4/4 Uvicorn+Chromium integration PASS**, with existing Quality approval/undo/restore/restart and project manager journeys. Frontend unchanged: [prior 145 Node + build + 5 Chromium](https://github.com/Sonkhe-Shongwe/PilotODIN/actions/runs/41840976435) remains scoped prior evidence only.

**P1 OPEN.** Fence is **not** universal for reports, experiments, investigations or background workers; no permanent Delete, native Windows tests, physical scaling, reduced motion, Narrator or release approval.
